crate_rows:\n        tier = (row.get(\"crate_tier\") or \"\").strip()\n        cost_raw = (row.get(\"crate_cost_usd\") or \"\").strip()\n        if not tier or not cost_raw:\n            continue\n        m[tier] = float(cost_raw)\n    return m\n\n\ndef compute_model(\n    *,\n    payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd: float,\n    active_clinics: int,\n    drug_cost_per_1000_doses_usd: float,\n    doses_per_day: float,\n    days_per_dispatch: int,\n    dispatches_per_year: int,\n    crate_cost_usd: float,\n) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    annual_revenue = payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd * active_clinics * dispatches_per_year\n    annual_drug_cost = (\n        drug_cost_per_1000_doses_usd\n        * active_clinics\n        * doses_per_day\n        * days_per_dispatch\n        * dispatches_per_year\n        / 1000.0\n    )\n\n    # IMPORTANT: Per task instruction, crate cost is per dispatch (not per clinic).\n    annual_crate_cost = crate_cost_usd * dispatches_per_year\n\n    annual_margin = annual_revenue - annual_drug_cost - annual_crate_cost\n\n    return {\n        \"days_per_dispatch\": days_per_dispatch,\n        \"dispatches_per_year\": dispatches_per_year,\n \"annual_margin_usd\": round2(annual_margin),\n    }\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    manifest = read_json(ROOT / \"campaign_manifest.json\")\n    crate_rows = read_csv_dicts(ROOT / \"crate_cost.csv\")\n require_nonempty(str(c.get(\"campaign_id\", \"\")), field=\"campaign_id\", context=\"manifest\")\n\n        billing_choice = resolve_billing_for_campaign(c, billing_rows)\n\n        active_clinics,       doses_per_day=doses_per_day,\n            days_per_dispatch=6,\n            dispatches_per_year=60,\n            crate_cost_usd=crate_cost_usd,\n        )\n        model_12 = compute_model(\n            payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd=billing_choice.payment_per_dispatch_per_clinic_usd,\n            active_clinics=active_clinics,\n            drug_cost_per_1000_doses_usd=drug_cost_per_1000,\n            doses_per_day=doses_per_day,\n            days_per_dispatch=12,\n            dispatches_per_year=30,\n            },\n                \"difference_margin_12_minus_6_usd\": diff,\n            }\n        )\n\n    out_campaigns.sort(key=lambda x: x[\"campaign_id\"])\n\n    total_margin_6 =
